## Mad Men in Minsk

2020-08-31 · The wild adventures of Vlad and Ollie

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/68a62452-ade7-40f1-8178-a46b7423dc28_1100x733.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!kXT_!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F68a62452-ade7-40f1-8178-a46b7423dc28_1100x733.jpeg)

[Vlad](https://twitter.com/VladDavidzon) has been tweeting up a storm lately about events in Minsk. The Paris/Odesa-based blogger popped up on the radar screen this spring in Kyiv as a potential candidate for COVID-19 superspreader of the year. He knows almost nothing about Belarus, which makes him an ideal candidate to opine on what’s happening there.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/33b832cd-b3e5-4772-90ee-204be62ee9c6_436x521.png)](https://twitter.com/ACEurasia/status/1299470052241145857)

Bluetick [Ollie](https://twitter.com/olliecarroll), meanwhile, writes for [The Independent](https://www.independent.co.uk/topic/Belarus) based in Moscow. From the looks of it, he has a room in the Mink Hotel overlooking Independence Square, from which he periodically posts ominous-looking photos of riot police deployments.

Both Vlad and Ollie reported on Russia’s invasion of Ukraine in early 2014, reinforcing the Kremlin narrative that pro-Russian rebels in eastern Ukraine had launched a popular insurrection. Fortunately, their dispatches from Minsk have been generally in line with reality, following the narrative purveyed by [The Guardian](https://www.theguardian.com/world/2020/aug/30/belarus-protesters-defy-crackdown-to-march-through-minsk-lukashenko) and [The New York Times](https://www.nytimes.com/2020/08/30/world/europe/belarus-lukashenko-protests.html?searchResultPosition=2) (blueticks [@INechepurenko](https://twitter.com/INechepurenko)  and [@antontroian](https://twitter.com/antontroian))

Follow.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/330f60d7-4550-4a1e-babf-048039d4a137_1280x1280.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!z2E9!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F330f60d7-4550-4a1e-babf-048039d4a137_1280x1280.jpeg)

Among local journalists, media trainer turned freelancer and researcher [Hanna](https://twitter.com/HannaLiubakova) has been prolific. She and Vlad were recently appointed non-resident fellows at the Atlantic Council’s U.S.-based lobbying firm/think tank. Unlike Vlad and Ollie, she is probably familiar with the works of Karatkevich, Adamovich, Bykov, Baradullin, Budinas, Alexievich, etc. The cultural baggage will enable her tease out and explain the historical and cultural roots of the popular tumult.

## Maria's Very Bad Day

2020-09-01 · Calling bullshit on would-be leader of Belarusian revolution

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/d2872d86-1a0d-49b8-99de-d1ae2de9f0c7_845x603.png)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!nGmq!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd2872d86-1a0d-49b8-99de-d1ae2de9f0c7_845x603.png)

Something has gone horribly wrong for Maria Kalesnikava, who over the weekend gave a [bullshit interview](https://www.kommersant.ru/doc/4474897#id1933741) with Maxim Znak to Kommersant flak Andrej Kolesnikov. It coincided with [a wacko 4-minute video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?time_continue=1&v=ynf9F0FOTa8&feature=emb_logo) posted by to YouTube account of Vikor Babariko, her nominal boss, who was thrown in jail and not allowed to run in the August 9, 2020 presidential election.

What happened? What was/is Maria thinking? Who does she think she is?

## Mouthpiece Experimentation Failure

2020-09-01 · Maria misses the sweet spot

In terms of the number of people pissed off, albeit for the wrong reasons, this announcement may set a record.

It’s all about floutist Maria Kаlesnikаva and banker Viktor Babariko (Viktar Babaryka), who on August 31 said in a YouTube video that they’ve decided to create a new political party/movement called Together (Vmeste).

The 4-minute long clip was pre-recorded, because Babariko and his son Eduard were detained by police on June 19, 2020 and thrown in jail. Last time I checked they were still there.

Kаlesnikаva helped run Babariko’s presidential campaign.

She is currently a member of the 7-member Presidium of the Coordinating Council seeking dialogue with self-proclaimed President Alexander Lukashenko. She is one of three remaining council members not behind bars.

Wikipedia (which on matters involving Belarus is often incorrect) [says](https://bit.ly/3bePXRT):

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e79f1da2-f99d-4125-a5d1-dff6a6f4a989_1046x307.png)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!BXCr!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe79f1da2-f99d-4125-a5d1-dff6a6f4a989_1046x307.png)

**Three basic popular demands have remained the same since protests erupted on August 9:**

1. Lukashenko must leave **ASAP**
2. All political prisoners must be released **immediately**
3. Form temporary government, hold new parliamentary and presidential elections according to 1994 Constitution

Maybe Maria and Viktor should use their energies to achieve these goals? Now. Or at least chat up the ideas with Svetlana Tikhanovskaya and her colleagues.

There will be plenty of time for political party registration games *after* Lukashenko is deposed and political prisoners have been released.

## Svetlana Reacts to YouTube Clip

2020-09-01 · Trouble in revolution paradise?

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/681bc78c-a550-41e1-a6c9-36c541e8f384_1200x841.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!96eZ!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F681bc78c-a550-41e1-a6c9-36c541e8f384_1200x841.jpeg)

Svitlana Tikhanovskaya has responded to the [weird YouTube video](https://bit.ly/3gUhNEs) posted by Floutist Maria Kalensnikava and would-be presidential candidate Viktor Babariko last night.

Tikhanovskaya said she doesn’t have a problem with Babriko and his supporters creating a political party.

At the same time, she picked out two statements made in the clip, with which she strongly disagreed.

> Firstly, I strongly disagree with the statement that we could not win the elections. Everyone knows that this is not the case. We won, but they are trying to steal our victory. That is why people came out to protest, that is why strikes began, that is why our entire society is protesting - from students to pensioners, from athletes to entrepreneurs
>
> Secondly, I do not agree that the first task facing us is constitutional reform. This goal setting does not meet the demands of the Belarusian people, and looks ambiguous, since today Lukashenko is trying to delay his departure, talking about an incomprehensible constitutional reform that will take place over an indefinite period of time, Tikhanovskaya [said](https://gomel.today/rus/news/belarus-16987/).

## Ukraine's COVID-19 Disaster Update

2020-09-02 · Brought to you by Ze!Team

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/be00757b-8fe6-49b3-8566-ccad11911811_609x408.png)](https://covid19.rnbo.gov.ua/?fbclid=IwAR1TXauFhOd3A9iOgaRCiZ27py5Ngz2hmDkaVuZj_7U-Oe3ejIQn34wBT9I)

It’s day whatever of Ukraine’s COVID-19 disaster and it’s obvious something has gone horribly wrong.

The [positive dynamics of morbidity](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/postive-dynamics-of-morbidity) in over the six months have been driven by [pathological bad thinking,](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/cataplectic-non-cognitivism) whacko algorithms, [formulations of set theory without variables](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/zeteams-replication-crisis), [kooky mitigation models for flattening the curve](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/ukraines-ugly-little-covid-19-secrets), [magical epidemic fantasies](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/magical-epidemic-thinking-22a), [irredeemable health officials](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/ukraines-irredeemable-health-ministry), [pandemic politics](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/zes-covid-clowns)and false assumptions promulgated by [poorly calibrated agents](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/what-makes-soup-soup) with [bad ideas](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/ukraines-human-sacrifice-experiment) who abandoned the [precautionary principle](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/the-precautionary-principle).

The result isn't unlike a horrible accident, except it’s been caused by an former comedian actor desperate for attention and applause. You want to laugh and be entertained, but you also want to avoid becoming a victim.

---

Recall what President Volodymyr Zelensky [said](https://www.president.gov.ua/en/news/glava-derzhavi-postaviv-nizku-zavdan-kabinetu-ministriv-z-me-59533?fbclid=IwAR0b_JJ8MvMwFhAxs3H5IMbzQYJ4TXvrTrLkK7CYG_e0R_wip2kvdrzzdzs) on January 31 about the pandemic.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/7a1e4377-8204-492a-b55d-f500f5096464_793x227.png)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!eia9!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F7a1e4377-8204-492a-b55d-f500f5096464_793x227.png)

## What Belarusians Need Least

2020-09-02 · Let's go back to the starting point

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e7f0e99d-5d56-4996-ac68-fad54220701b_1276x956.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!5vQ9!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe7f0e99d-5d56-4996-ac68-fad54220701b_1276x956.jpeg)

I just noticed that some foreign Belarus experts and journalists don’t have a blue ticks, so, technically, you don't need to respect them.

The revolution has lasted 25 days so far, more than enough time to make several observations.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/5630c220-fad2-4ce1-b230-4084e3a4be09_1032x581.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!jCNb!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5630c220-fad2-4ce1-b230-4084e3a4be09_1032x581.jpeg)

The first is that the question “**Who wins?”** is self-undermining at this stage. The second is Belarusians have already accomplished a lot without much outside help, and the third is keeping it this way is probably a good idea.

**We can conclude that Belarusians do not require:**

1. expert advice
2. money
3. pencils and oranges

Taking them in reverse order, writing instruments abound in Belarus, a country with plenty of books, fresh fruit and Internet connectivity. Money is also not in short supply. No one is going to die of hunger. Outside expert advice, especially from Moscow, Kyiv and Washington, about how best to depose the bloody dictator should be disregarded politely out of a deep sense of self-loathing.


---

## Dialogue with Lukashenko

2020-09-03 · Why we can't all just get along

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/80cd7c54-0cfc-4e1f-8f9f-d0edb19fdf3e_843x939.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!X_rk!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F80cd7c54-0cfc-4e1f-8f9f-d0edb19fdf3e_843x939.jpeg)

The uneasy alliance is finally breaking down - so which is it gonna be, facts or logic?

Alexander Lukashenko is currently the Belarusian president who, according to international law, *lost* the August 9 election and whose term of office expires November 5, 2020.

He is *unpopular* and would lose a repeat presidential election, if one could be arranged within **the next 64 days**. That’s why he and Russian officials are now calling for changes to be made to the Constitution instead.

The creation two weeks ago of the Coordinating Council representing civil society has been instructive, not least because the exercise illustrates the **impossibility** of dialogue between Lukashenko and his critics in Belarus.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e7983965-c0a4-41a6-830d-e6502cf5c898_899x600.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!PfO0!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe7983965-c0a4-41a6-830d-e6502cf5c898_899x600.jpeg)

As of this writing, four of the seven members of the council’s presidium are in prison or traveling abroad. Of the remaining three, Maria Kalesnikava and Maksym Znak, have stopped making sense. That leaves Svetlana Alexievich, who has said Putin might help.

Putin won’t help.

**November 5, 2020. Mark the date in your calendar.**

## A Very Uncomfortable Place

2020-09-04 · Blue-ticked journalists flee Belarus

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/dbc754fd-4d96-41b5-beb5-c8724da2f1ac_1241x576.png)](https://nbcnews.to/2DwxzYq)

MSNBC’s Chief Foreign Correspondent Richard Engel has disappeared from my Belarusian radar screen. Perhaps he and his team exfiltrated themselves to Lithuania across the Neman River?

Their epic reportage about Lukashenko’s last days in power is like a skillful action movie about a plot that exists only to support a skillful action movie.

Towards the end of the 11-minute story there comes a point when we realize there will be no higher level to the screenplay than talking stealthily over cocktails with cool edgy women not wearing masks in an old industrial neighborhood on the outskirts of Minsk.

The [Wild Adventures of Vlad and Ollie](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/mad-men-in-minsk) in Minsk may be finished, as well.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/0d0ef89a-4c4e-4396-887f-0eb61be7216b_673x380.png)](https://twitter.com/VladDavidzon/status/1301626374013308929)

Ollie, meanwhile, hasn’t [tweeted](https://twitter.com/olliecarroll) anything original about Belarus for days.

Blue-ticked journalists are in increasingly low supply.

## Bizarre Belarus Bullshit

2020-09-07 · Three wacko interviews designed to make you dizzy

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/d6a71981-1225-4503-b9bf-310f70b9377c_747x465.png)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ahZ8!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd6a71981-1225-4503-b9bf-310f70b9377c_747x465.png)

Ukraine’s equivalent to [Bernard-Henri Levy](https://twitter.com/BHL) is [Dmytro Gordon](https://twitter.com/dmitry_gordon), who is widely known in Kyiv as the [Golden Pyramid Guy](https://medium.com/@abdymok/ze-and-the-golden-pyramid-guy-d486b34ba0e9). [![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/dee659cd-e7fc-43a0-ad02-1989a867a0f2_870x596.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!h-L0!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fdee659cd-e7fc-43a0-ad02-1989a867a0f2_870x596.jpeg)

In it Dr. Peter Gross of the University of Tennessee at Knoxville channeled self-proclaimed Belarusian dissident Oleh Manaev.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/d52ae341-a26e-4212-8b70-f1bcac289dcd_433x606.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!mGrT!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd52ae341-a26e-4212-8b70-f1bcac289dcd_433x606.jpeg)

Professor Gross is retired and knows nothing about Belarus, except what he has learned from Oleh, who is not a Belarusian dissident.

## Thoughtcrime Up 8% in Belarus

2020-09-07 · The instant of decision is madness

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/a6e683be-0867-4f8c-a0ba-d523886a00b7_1280x853.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!nsek!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa6e683be-0867-4f8c-a0ba-d523886a00b7_1280x853.jpeg)

Attempts to revive social cohesion between Russia and Belarus seem as far-fetched today as they did a couple of months ago.

The abduction of Coordinating Council member Maria Kalesnikava on September 7 portends a slide towards something unrecognizably grotesque, making it too optimistic to hypothesize that a peaceful denouement is in the offing, the type of outcome EU officials say they hope for.

Putin and Lukashenko will likely discuss the *structure of criminality* in Belarus when the two dictators meet in Moscow in the coming days.

According to Belarusian Investigative Committee Chairman Ivan Noskevich, there’s been a drop in ordinary mischief this year, compared to 2019. However, he noted a significant uptick in *information-related crimes* over the same period*.*

This should reminds us that Foucault famously described Derrida as an *intellectual terrorist*, claiming the latter didn’t understand his gibberish because he was an idiot.

No one knows how many kidnappings and casualties will be required before Lukashenko and his henchmen are deposed.

We can count on blue-ticked foreign journalists in Moscow to get crucial things wrong with respect to the current madness. It was unsettling and mildly annoying in August to read their disanologies with Russian interventions in Georgia and Ukraine.

There is kind of a moral panic today to much of what is going on and too few level-headed observations from people familiar with Karatkevich, Adamovich and Bykov. Or even N.R.M.


---

## Cancel Lukashenko Culture

2020-09-08 · Things getting out of hand at the dictator reveal party

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/c8a2d526-ca50-4240-bf23-29276d21992f_481x485.png)](https://www.instagram.com/p/CE2APIfHX0a/)

([@vovatsesler](https://www.instagram.com/p/CE2APIfHX0a/))

The last time I checked, more than 10,000 Belarusians [had been arrested](https://spring96.org/be) for participating in demonstrations against dictator Alexander Lukashenko. Hundreds have been tortured and dozens recognized as political prisoners of his regime, which is being propped up by despots in Moscow.

**The demands of protesters are not complicated:**

1. Lukashenko must leave
2. Political prisoners must be released
3. New elections must be held

Belarus is a sovereign, independent country and its citizens can sort out the mess **without outside interference**. The last thing they need is a bunch of mask-wearing goons imported from Russia to maintain order or advice from Donald Trump.

## Svetlana Alexievich Statement, errata

2020-09-09 · Miscellaneous Belarus-related news

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/9a14a2ab-c160-4a7e-bb99-779c053e9047_496x744.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!wSOK!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9a14a2ab-c160-4a7e-bb99-779c053e9047_496x744.jpeg)

Last remaining member of the Coordinating Council Presidium in Belarus Svetlana Alexievich [released a statement](https://pen-centre.by/en/2020/09/09/3779.html) on September 9, 2020.

Here’s a quick xlation:

> My like-minded friends in the Presidium of the Coordinating Council are no longer there. Everyone is either in jail or expelled. Today they detained the last member, Maksym Znak.
>
> First, they kidnapped the country and now they are kidnapping the best of us. Hundreds of others will replace us. It was not the Coordination Council that revolted. The country revolted. I want to repeat what I always say. We were not preparing a coup. We wanted to prevent a split in our country. We wanted a dialogue to begin in society. Lukashenko says he will not speak to the “street,” which is hundreds of thousands of people who go out every Sunday and every day. This is not a street literally. This is the people.
>
> People go out with their young children because they believe they will win.
>
> I also want to appeal to the Russian intelligentsia, the term we used in the past. Why are you silent? We only hear rare voices of support. Why are you silent when you see a small, proud people being trampled? We are still your brothers.
>
> And I want to tell my people that I love them. I'm proud of them.
>
> Here again, a stranger is ringing my doorbell ...
>
> [Svetlana Alexeivich](https://www.nobelprize.org/prizes/literature/2015/alexievich/facts/),
>
> Nobel laureate, head of the Belarusian PEN center

---

***Dementors** (Хапуны,* var. *) are masked creatures widely considered to be one of the foulest to inhabit Belarus. They feed on human happiness and generate feelings of depression and despair in any person in close proximity to them. They are often referred to as "soul-suckers" and are closely associated with [Titushki](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Titushky#:~:text=Titushky%20(plural%3B%20Ukrainian%3A%20%D1%82%D1%96%D1%82%D1%83%D1%88%D0%BA%D0%B8,purpose%20of%20performing%20illegal%20acts.), mercenary agents who supported the riot police during the Ukraine’s Revolution of Dignity in 2013-2014.*

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/1d33863b-e903-4b75-906a-9151219de489_735x851.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!GC7B!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1d33863b-e903-4b75-906a-9151219de489_735x851.jpeg)

---

---

Belarusian poet Vladimir Neklyaev, meanwhile, had this to say in an [interview](https://belaruspartisan.by/politic/511719/) published by Belarusian Partizan on September 9, 2020.

> “[**Lukashenko**] is full of fear. He is consumed by hatred and a thirst for revenge. Fear, hatred and revenge make up his entire essence. His father was not Belarusian, and he never liked Belarus.
>
> He especially disliked Minsk, which was initially too ‘aware’ for him. And now, after the whole of Belarus has become too "aware" for him, he has grabbed a machine gun out of fear and hatred.
>
> Why? To exact revenge!
>
> For what reason? Because both Minsk and Belarus have denied him power, the authority he, after deceiving the people, ascribed to himself in the Constitution and considered eternal. Why isn't he shooting yet? He is not shoot only because he lacks sufficient forces to deploy against Minsk and the entire country,” Neklyaev [said](https://belaruspartisan.by/politic/511719/).

## With All Due Respect

2020-09-10 · Goon squad raids Coordinating Council in Minsk

It is truly fascinating how the differences between blueticks and their native counterparts revolve around \*which\* specific narratives of stories they are weaving rather than what is actually taking place.

> **Europe’s last dictator has ruled Belarus with an iron fist since 1994.**

This is a sentence from from an otherwise uninteresting [2,500-word article](https://bit.ly/33cdjUE) about exiled Belarusian Svetlana Tikhanovskaya published in Buzzfeed. A Google search for “Lukashenko” + “iron” produces hundreds of thousands of matches for the boilerplate trope.

There have been many cases throughout history where catastrophic failures have occurred when iron was below the ductile to brittle transition temperature.

---

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/d99fb5cd-9584-488e-aed9-ce1ea24510ba_1597x898.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Mipy!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd99fb5cd-9584-488e-aed9-ce1ea24510ba_1597x898.jpeg)

(Lenin Square, Minsk. April 4, 1991)

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/607a6db0-1d0f-43e9-bead-c80c5e170113_700x470.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!43lD!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F607a6db0-1d0f-43e9-bead-c80c5e170113_700x470.jpeg)

---

**An unrelated example of messy thinking and a failure to communicate:**

> "Ukraine has carefully studied both shellings and sees them as two distinct cases of provocative intent. We demand that Russia intervene and influence the provocative behavior of illegal armed groups in Donetsk and Luhansk region in order to maintain the ceasefire," the President’s Office [said](https://www.ukrinform.ua/rubric-polytics/3094567-ukraina-vimagae-vid-rosii-vplinuti-na-provokativni-dii-nzf-na-donbasi-op.html?fbclid=IwAR2jBateoDkf4-khc_TTbTPOI6yWWJHerPcDhpblAja6jBM6kpEkxNY7Pfg).

The last time I checked, Ukraine was fighting Russia-occupation forces in Donetsk and Luhansk regions. Now Ukraine’s government says it is fighting \***illegal armed groups**\* there and wants Russia to intervene.

## Batshit Ukrainian Diplomacy

2020-09-11 · Belarusian opposition briefs FM Kuleba

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/f251d2ab-633b-4f6c-9996-4a08a8e7836d_1200x675.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!fZ4A!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff251d2ab-633b-4f6c-9996-4a08a8e7836d_1200x675.jpeg)

Ukrainian Foreign Minister Dmytro Kuleba [blurted out](https://bit.ly/3inPksa) in an interview published on September 8, 2020 that he wanted to hear the position of the Belarusian opposition on the development future Ukraine-Belarus relations, about Russia’s aggression against Ukraine, annexation of Crimea, Russia’s war in Donbas, etc.

**As if he did not know already**.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/83382f25-b9d0-48fb-8b2d-b6b62f7383c4_647x298.png)](https://bit.ly/3inPksa)

Andrei Sannikov [obliged](https://www.facebook.com/andrei.sannikov.1/posts/3703773556354290?fbclid=IwAR18JBk__9IpyATi2XzF4iQcD3Uf8bxbq6iSz8GwICG-T_jzIiPt34BY2sk) on September 11.

> **ANSWER TO MINISTER OF FOREIGN AFFAIRS OF UKRAINE D. KULEBA**
>
> Recently, Ukrainian Foreign Minister Dmytro Kuleba, whom I respect, publicly asked about the attitude of the Belarusian opposition to Ukraine, Russian aggression, illegal occupation of Ukrainian territories and annexation of Crimea.
>
> The question, I think, is rhetorical, but if you need an answer, then here it is:
>
> The Belarusian opposition unconditionally supports the territorial integrity of Ukraine, condemns Russian aggression, does not recognize the annexation of Crimea and considers it Ukrainian.
>
> If someone says otherwise, then this is not the opposition and not Belarusian.
>
> Moreover, on some issues we take an even more principled position than the Ukrainian authorities, for example, on the Minsk talks. It was obvious to us from the very beginning that negotiations for peace conducted on the territory of the murderer (Alexander Lukashenko) were doomed to failure, and that to continue them now is a crime.
>
> I, in turn, also have questions to the foreign ministry of Ukraine, the state on which the future of our region largely depends:
>
> Why are you ashamed to support the democratic forces of Belarus? Why do you declare your adherence to the ideals of democracy only to your Western partners, while in Belarus you are dealing primarily with the regime? The regime that supports the annexation of Crimea, promotes war in eastern Ukraine, conducts maneuvers that threaten international peace on the territory of Belarus and has long had no legitimacy.
>
> Why has the foreign ministry of Ukraine been so shy about meeting with the Belarusian opposition recently, to demonstrate adherence to the principles of democracy?
>
> Previously it was different. For example, one of my first meetings, as a representative of the “shadow government” of Hennadiy Karpenko, I held with the Minister of Foreign Affairs of Ukraine, legendary Hennadiy Udovenko, and I met my friend and colleague Kostiantyn Ivanovych Hryshchenko many times.
>
> Don't be embarrassed. You are a powerful country, the cornerstone, as the leading political scientists of the world say, not only of security, but also democratic development of a vast region.
>
> Ukraine today could play an important role in normalizing the situation in Belarus, ending violence, supporting the democratic forces and the people of our country.
>
> Glory to Ukraine!
>
> Long live Belarus!
>
> [Andrei Sannikov](https://www.facebook.com/andrei.sannikov.1/posts/3703773556354290?fbclid=IwAR18JBk__9IpyATi2XzF4iQcD3Uf8bxbq6iSz8GwICG-T_jzIiPt34BY2sk)

## Там Нас Нет

2020-09-12 · It leads when it bleeds

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/db911a4e-f0c3-45d4-a70a-658413e9bad4_1200x799.jpeg)

At least four - and probably many more - English-language journalists say Putin’s meeting with Lukashenko in Sochi on Monday could determine the outcome of the revolution in Belarus.

That’s a problem, because **western journalists are not there**.

---

[Putin Holds the Keys to Belarus Crisis](https://www.theguardian.com/world/2020/sep/11/putin-holds-key-to-belarus-crisis-as-lukashenko-heads-to-moscow), Shaun Walker for The Guardian:

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/333f38df-546f-4a53-82da-06c00b53c046_578x210.png)

[Crushing Democracy in Belarus,](https://www.tabletmag.com/sections/news/articles/crushing-democracy-belarus-lukashenko?fbclid=IwAR0FGW0cT2GTQ-iMKXpcew6HJwN484dlGFL3OODxMqGaJkBap-g_A0c0WU0) Vlad Davidzon, Atlantic Council expert:

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/ca9a396e-629e-4205-93f4-d6dc0fa999f1_821x276.png)

[What’s the Endgame for Belarus?](https://www.nytimes.com/2020/09/12/world/europe/belarus-russia-protests-endgame.html) Andrew Higgins for The New York Times:

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/c2c5d167-321a-47ea-96ff-0283b7555672_756x203.png)

[Belarus Will Never Be the Same Again](https://www.csmonitor.com/World/Europe/2020/0911/With-or-without-Lukashenko-Belarus-will-never-be-the-same-again), Fred Weir for The Christian Science Monitor:

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/f767a07b-55a2-4387-bffc-3e48a45f6c87_619x180.png)

---

Russophile and Sovietophile interpolations of events in Belarus are passé. What happens next **does not hinge on** what Putin and Lukashenko talk about in Sochi. The next chapter of Belarusian history **will not be written in Moscow**. Decoys, deflections, pins and interference are unlikely to spook people who could care less about what the Kremlin has concluded.

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/90a53750-f03a-4e80-b1c8-183259e8cb7d_1080x1069.jpeg)

## Pants on Fire

2020-09-14 · Destabilization in your trousers

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/8afe265d-400e-4038-b0d5-c7d5cc611d07_1200x799.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!SlD2!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F8afe265d-400e-4038-b0d5-c7d5cc611d07_1200x799.jpeg)

*Lukashenko is finished. So is Putin.*

---

It’s shocking what you can get away with, as long as you don’t dress or act like a hipster. Just ask Vladimir Putin and Alexander Lukashenko, who rue the implosion of the USSR. Both men have played the “big man, strong man” role for decades and are unconcerned with appearing trendy.

No documents will be signed at their meeting on Monday in Sochi and there will be no press conference. The chit chat will be hush hush.

The reason is is because both Putin and Lukashenko are scared shitless.

:-)

✔️ Talks constructive and rich in content.

✔️ Lukashenko will either flee or be deposed.

✔️ Russia will be forced to re-think its Belarus strategy.

✔️ There will be no constitutional reform while Lukashenko remains president.

✔️ Calls to release political prisoners will grow louder.

✔️ OSCE is unable/incapable of playing a meaningful role in mitigating disaster.

## The Disappeared

2020-09-16 · Lukashenko opponents who went missing

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/099f81a6-375a-4019-9a9e-74daf245f44a_620x217.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0PvJ!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F099f81a6-375a-4019-9a9e-74daf245f44a_620x217.jpeg)

On September 16, 1999, former Belarusian Deputy Prime Minister and ex-head of the Central Election Commission Viktor Gonchar and his friend, businessman Anatoly Krasovsky, disappeared in Minsk.

In May 1999, ex-head of the Belarusian Ministry of Internal Affairs Yuriy Zakharenko, disappeared. In the summer of 2000, ORT cameraman Dmitry Zavadsky disappeared.

## Frogs for Dinner

2020-09-17 · Logical, rational conversations are not on the menu

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/816d502e-571f-478f-b373-83964285ce00_1237x645.png)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!8Xkp!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F816d502e-571f-478f-b373-83964285ce00_1237x645.png)

Breaking down large communities of Lukashenko loyalists into smaller, more soluble groupings that can be digested and re-absorbed into Belarusian civil society will take time and lots of patience. You can’t get rid of all the passionate sadistic hatred all at once.

It’s easy to be anti-realist against Belarus, because enemies of democracy are scheming against changes and rationalizing the dictatorship, if only to justify their own servility for a quarter of a century. They will claim human rights are a conspiracy and that the Internet really is just a tool to foist the correspondence theory of truth on everyone.

That’s where deflationism comes in: downplay everything and enjoy pretty sunsets.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/17350290-25cd-4ccb-80ca-4d5a619989f3_1280x720.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!w4VY!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F17350290-25cd-4ccb-80ca-4d5a619989f3_1280x720.jpeg)

cf. [Taras Taraikovsky List](https://nashaniva.by/?c=ar&i=259205)

## Moscow Mechanism Mumbling

2020-09-18 · Authority to stamp whatever myths

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/f21b9b22-b3db-46f1-8358-894fb2aef39d_770x512.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!d0pf!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff21b9b22-b3db-46f1-8358-894fb2aef39d_770x512.jpeg)

There has been a lot of chatter recently about getting the Organization for Security and Cooperation in Europe to send a bunch of rapporteurs to Belarus to investigate allegations of widespread human rights abuses. The plan is to use the the [Moscow Mechanism](https://bit.ly/3iIo33M), a diplomatic tool for discovering whether political prisoners are still alive. Or dead.

To date, the Moscow Mechanism has been used six times:

- **1992:** By the then 12 states of the European Community and the United States concerning attacks on civilians in Croatia and Bosnia Herzegovina. The mission completed a visit to Croatia in October 1992, but was unable to go to Bosnia because of the lack of adequate security.
- **1992:** By Estonia (self-invoked) to study Estonian legislation concerning citizenship and languages. 1993: By Moldova (self-invoked) to examine legislation and policies concerning minorities' rights. The Mission considered and commented on laws on language, citizenship, and religious freedom.
- **1993:** By a consensus decision of the OSCE Committee of Senior Officials to investigate human rights violations in Serbia and Montenegro, particularly the cases of Vuk and Danica Drašković.
- **2003:** By 10 OSCE participating States (Germany, United States, Austria, Canada, United Kingdom, Greece, Ireland, Italy, Norway, Sweden) in relation to Turkmenistan to examine human rights concerns in the wake of the reported attack on 25 November 2002 on President Niyazov and subsequent investigation and arrests.
- **2011:** By 14 OSCE participating States (the Czech Republic, the United States, Germany, Canada, Denmark, Finland, the United Kingdom, Iceland, Norway, Netherlands, Poland, Romania, Slovakia, and Sweden) concerning human rights in Belarus following presidential elections in December 2010.

## Balaclavas Inside Out

2020-09-21 · Wildly fantastic and frequently absurd

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/7ac8e061-9ee7-4de8-9fd7-58ee741676ad_1200x798.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ewiD!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F7ac8e061-9ee7-4de8-9fd7-58ee741676ad_1200x798.jpeg)

(***Demonstrator from the Women’s March held in Minsk on September 19, 2020. The sign reads, “Balaclavas Inside Out***)

Andrei Kolesnikov at the Moscow Carnegie Center has written an [interesting article](https://carnegie.ru/commentary/82723) about the revolution underway in Belarus.

He opines that the confrontation with everything official there is designed to ridicule authorities, who long ago became ridiculous in their super-serious attitudes towards themselves.

> [The events] resemble a merry carnival exposing an already caricatured dictator as a naked emperor, *roi pour rire*, a king to be laughed at, Kolesnikov [said](https://carnegie.ru/commentary/82723).

Which brings us to [Mikhail Bakhtin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mikhail_Bakhtin), a philosopher of language, who last century in Vitebsk thought a lot about about ***thinking the essence of the word in its correspondence to being***.

Kolesnikov says what’s going on in Belarus today indeed resembles a ***carnival***, which is the opposite of official ceremonies and actions, which seemed to have migrated from the Middle Ages to modern post-Soviet autocracies, in this case, Belarus.

> “The official holiday, in essence, looked only back, into the past, and with this past it sanctified the existing system. The official holiday, sometimes even contrary to its own idea, asserted the stability, immutability and eternity of the entire existing world order: the existing hierarchy, existing religious, political and moral values, norms, and prohibitions. The holiday was a triumph of the already finished, victorious, ruling truth, which appeared as an eternal, unchanging and indisputable truth. Therefore, the tone of an official holiday could only be monolithic and serious." (Bakhtin. *[Rabelais and Folk Culture of the Middle Ages and Renaissance](http://www.bim-bad.ru/docs/bakhtin_rablai.pdf).*)

---

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/b36835fa-8bb6-467a-8c08-e9a23cd30aa1_1080x1080.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!r0XB!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fb36835fa-8bb6-467a-8c08-e9a23cd30aa1_1080x1080.jpeg)

Bakhtin wrote that ***carnival*** personifies the oppositional culture of the oppressed, of a ‘world turned inside out,’ where ordinary citizens engage in behavior that mocks law enforcers and sets new fashion trends.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/f7b5ff9e-7265-47d8-9f79-9c291fb53d9f_727x982.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Vmrm!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff7b5ff9e-7265-47d8-9f79-9c291fb53d9f_727x982.jpeg)

**A picture tells a thousand words.**

Kolesnikov says demonstrators in Belarus are clearly aware of the derisive, political meaning of their outfits and placards, which demonstrate intentionally a strictly anti-paternalistic orientation.

> "A coffin for the dictatorship - We are depriving the father of paternal rights," reads one sign. “Help from Moscow, Kremlin contacts? - ВВП (Vladimir Vladimirovich Putin) [GDP] is something to be raised, not someone to call,” says another. A third reads “Error 404. Democracy not found.”

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/5db3bdc2-9341-408c-8f37-fd802c805e74_924x615.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!UesF!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5db3bdc2-9341-408c-8f37-fd802c805e74_924x615.jpeg)

Signs poke fun of riot police using slang in the familiar \*you\* form. Some protesters wear colored masks and fake police uniforms with profane labels on their backs.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/042e0c8d-6a5c-49d8-8169-a349eef539f3_1253x868.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!DWLB!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F042e0c8d-6a5c-49d8-8169-a349eef539f3_1253x868.jpeg)

Many placards call attention to the fact that Minsk looks a lot better since drug addicts and prostitutes began marching in the streets almost two months ago.

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/a6451409-f51c-47a9-8cbc-150bf11fff8a_934x716.jpeg)](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Je7f!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa6451409-f51c-47a9-8cbc-150bf11fff8a_934x716.jpeg)

The above sign is addressed to Lukashenko. It reads, “You love to be like Hitler. Enjoy and blow your brains out!”

According to Bakhtin, the basic condition governing the production of meaning in all discourses can be split into a series of interacting metalanguages, sometimes conflicting, sometimes at play. Bakhtin called this interaction ***polyglossia.***

[![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e3e001ff-5024-455b-876b-591ec4f71aa2_607x339.png)](https://link.springer.com/chapter/10.1057%2F9781403978226_1)

---

```
Dasein (being there) is a German word. Being There is also the title of an American comedy-drama film adapted from a 1970 novel by Jerzy Kosiński.
```

```
Being in the World would be the Heideggerian title.
```

```
Carneval is Karneval in German, also known as Fasching, when people in some parts of Germany traditionally break the rules, poke fun at those who make the rules and then make their own new rules.
```

## A Cyprian's Supper

2020-09-22 · This whole is not a seamless coneness

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/877e4bd8-df8d-47b2-bb47-69c777a43e66_1079x613.jpeg)

We left off [yesterday](https://abdymok.substack.com/p/balaclavas-inside-out) talking about what *Dasein (In-der-Welt-sein)* means à la  [Heidegger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martin_Heidegger) and recommending [Kolesnikov’s article](https://carnegie.ru/commentary/82723) that compares the ongoing insurrection in Belarus to *Carnival* (*Karneval)* - as interpolated by philosopher of language [Mikhail Bakhtin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mikhail_Bakhtin).

> The folk tradition of the carnival is preserved: according to custom, girls bring pumpkins as a sign that they do not want to marry a disloyal one. The disliked one is Lukashenko or a very specific security official standing like an idol without a face. "Sasha, you are our ex!" “You can't be cute by force!” “Sasha, we need to leave. It's about you." In general, the Belarusian protest has a woman's face: this is exactly what Lukashenko was moved by during his meeting with Putin in Sochi, even though he earlier used iron cages and a Kalashnikov rifle without a cartridge to protect himself against women, Kolesnikov [wrote](https://carnegie.ru/commentary/82723).

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/7b3005e0-fa56-4741-ade6-9557bbe4eaee_473x651.jpeg)

But back to Bakhtin. He studied history and philosophy in Odesa before relocating to Nevel (Pskov region) in 1918. He moved two years later to Vitebsk, which at the turn of the 20th century was a small town of about 65,000 people, half of whom were Jews. The town was a hotbed of intellectual and artistic experimentation that gave birth to a new kind of aesthetics and a significant impulse of post-revolutionary modernism that began with art and paralleled shifts in Neo-Kantian philosophy, linguistics, semiotics and culture. (Black Square, Bakhtin Circle, etc, ad nauseum).

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/bd18a781-9b74-4ac7-a5e3-66843b6328b0_1200x1204.jpeg)

*Liebensphilosophie* - Art as life, as it were.

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/5a1ad519-dc34-464f-9094-ceb7aa0f3239_595x129.png)

---

One century later, we’re left with this:

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/f64cf1e7-7233-4dff-b4d5-9040c48a788b_812x446.jpeg)

cf. [Cena Cypriani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cena_Cypriani)

## Beyond the pale

2020-10-05 · Черта́ осе́длости

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/355d6f5c-c68f-47eb-8fca-7917f7f192e6_1076x1078.jpeg)

Egalitarian politics is ever a mixture of leveling up and leveling down. Attempts to focus exclusively on one poll will fail, because they’re both important.

Z is solidly in the leveling-down camp. He and his lieutenants have kept Ukraine outside Europe’s pale for more than a year.

Pales for centuries have been enforced in various European countries for political reasons, notably in Ireland (the Pale of Dublin), France (the Pale of Calais) and later Russia (the Pale of Settlement).

The term 'pale' (*черта́ осе́длости*) means literally the area enclosed by such fence and figuratively 'the area that is enclosed and safe.' To be beyond it means being outside the area accepted as 'home.'

Z in late August said his country’s ‘home’ is in Europe.

> "I have told many European leaders that they should tell Ukrainians what steps we need to take for EU membership,” Zelensky [said](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uPDn55u2Xs8) in an interview with Euronews.

The president views critics as obstacles to his higher popularity rating. His rejection of *mere civility* ― a minimal, occasionally contemptuous adherence to culturally contingent rules of respectful behavior ― keeps Ukraine beyond Europe’s pale.

Even worse, dialectical discourse under Z is dead. There is no difference between *parrhesia* and *isegoria* in Ukraine, since reality is no longer expressed in rational categories.

Under Z, Ukraine is a sham democracy, a primitive post-soviet feudal autocracy that is not underpinned by elections or coordinated work of institutions, but by popular uprisings. Absent them, even the illusion of tolerance would not exist.

European Council President Charles Michel and European Commission President Ursula von der Leyen should ‘level up’ with Z during their meeting on October 6 in Brussels.

---

> '***If there is a pale, decent people stay inside it,***' a line from John Harington's lyric poem *[The History of Polindor and Flostella](https://books.google.com.ua/books/about/The_History_of_Polindor_and_Flostella.html?id=070enQEACAAJ&redir_esc=y)* printed in 1657.

---

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/262de49d-a1e9-4fc0-b70e-1133ff98bfb1_531x541.png)

---

Which brings us to the Zollman Effect, which posits that groups with more network connections will be generically less likely to arrive at a correct consensus. The group needs to entertain all the possible options long enough to gather good evidence and settle on the best one. In tightly connected networks, misleading evidence is widely shared, and may cause the community to pre-emptively settle on poor theories. Like in Ukraine under President Volodymyr Zelensky.

2020-11-14 · Random thoughts about punching someone in the face

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/9bc19fc6-0740-46a5-bb4b-f774ec3a1389_555x457.jpeg)

Just read Arkady Babchenko’s latest [Facebook post](https://www.facebook.com/babchenkoa/posts/3033603923406487) about the futility of demonstrating against bloody dictator Alexander Lukashenko. He delivered the same peaceful-resistance-is-pointless message earlier with much fanfare, conjuring up pink ponies and his personal experiences in Russia, Ukraine and various other hell holes.

> “If we are already talking about life and death, then personally I would prefer to die on the barricades with a Molotov cocktail, and not be pummeled unarmed in a police van. Actually, that is why I moved from the country of paddy wagons to the country of barricades,” he [writes](https://www.facebook.com/babchenkoa/posts/3033603923406487).

I didn’t see Arkady on the barricades in Kyiv on February 20, 2014, but I’m sure he was there, if only in spirit.

---

I was never a fan of forceful retaliation until I was mugged.

> The literal translation of the last lines of Antigone (1350-54) is as follows: "But great words, counteracting [or paying back] the great blows of the overproud, teach understanding in old age." ([Holderlin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Friedrich_H%C3%B6lderlin)'s translation: "*Grosse Blicke aber,* / *Grosse Streiche der hohen Schultern* / *Vergeltend*, / *Sie haben im Alter gelehrt, zu denken*.")

Sheer violence is mute. It might save your life, but violence alone can never be great, even when beating off muggers on S train.

Back in Sophocle’s day, the whole concept of civil disobedience, rule and being ruled, of government and power, belonged in the private rather than the public sphere. That has changed. Now equality - not justice - epitomizes liberty: to be free means to be free from the *inequality* present in rulership and to live in a place where people neither rule nor are ruled, like in modern day Ukraine, parts of Alberta and homesteads in northern Utah.

![](https://bucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/65252035-2a72-45f9-a1a5-adcb59f0f010_900x700.jpeg)

Secret state security services these days are walking a tightrope. Any miscalculation, could result in a hilarious failure, such as the recent botched attempt to liquidate Alexey Navalny, or a bloody mess, like the [Battle of Kassam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_of_Khasham).

OPSEC fuck ups in the United States and former Soviet states in recent years have been manifold. They demonstrate nothing is more corrosive than official anonymity and TOP SECRET security clearances. Not leaving digital clues has become harder, further complicating the enterprise of carrying out clandestine fuckery without without getting caught.

Julian Assange and Eduard Snowden a decade ago warned about Big Brother wanting to monitor our phone calls and track our movements. The information age has instead allowed anyone with a computer and a decent Internet connection to keep track of would-be guardians of the state. Shadowing inept operatives has never been easier or more entertaining.

Which brings us to Ukraine’s state security services and their year-long operation against Russian agents and Wagner PMC (Private Military Company). The plan was to force an emergency landing of the contractor's plane from Minsk in July 2020 as it flew through Ukrainian airspace. The mercenaries - several of whom had fought in eastern Ukraine - would have been arrested. Russian President Putin said the stunt was the brainchild of a joint U.S.-Ukrainian intelligence operation. The President’s Office of Ukraine denies any such operation existed.
